===== RUN 130: online_ip current-counter writers =====
Kernel current counters established by get_online_ip_info mapping:
  +0x50/+0x54 = current upload bytes
  +0x58/+0x5c = previous upload snapshot
  +0x60/+0x64 = current download bytes
  +0x68/+0x6c = previous download snapshot

===== MODULES IMPORTING online_ip SYMBOLS =====

--- firmware-src/RootFS-unpacked/lib/modules/bm.ko ---
         U clear_online_ip_flag
         U online_ip_hash
         U online_ip_hash_size

         U online_ip_hash_size

--- firmware-src/RootFS-unpacked/lib/modules/ip_filter.ko ---
